\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)).

for
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\in\mathscr{S}
. Due to Lemma
C.3
and the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{n}
, we directly have

\displaystyle T_{1,3,k}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\rightarrow 0
, for
\displaystyle k=1,3
, in probability under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}
, uniformly in
\displaystyle(\beta,\sigma)\in\Theta_{\beta}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
. We also have
\displaystyle T_{1,3,2}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\rightarrow 0
, in probability under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}
, due to the result in the following Lemma.

Lemma C.7
.

Let
\displaystyle f:\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\Theta\to\mathbb{R}
be a function satisfying the regularity assumption of Lemma
C.3
and
\displaystyle d_{R}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d
. Under conditions (
H2
) and (
H5
)–(
H8
), it holds that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty,\;\Delta\to 0,\;n\Delta\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}f({X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma^{\dagger}))}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0;

(C.12)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{f({X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma^{\dagger}))}{\sqrt{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma^{\dagger}))}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\biggr\}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

(C.13)

uniformly in
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
and
\displaystyle\lambda\in[0,1]
.

We will give the proof of Lemma
C.7
in Section
C.2.5
.

Proof of (
C.10
).

From (
A
), we have

\textstyle{\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{l=1}^{5}\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(l)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)},

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(1)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(2)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\cdot\frac{\mu_{i-1}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{i-1}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta)}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{2}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(3)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\cdot\frac{\mu_{i-1}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{i-1}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta)}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{1}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(4)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{\mu_{i-1}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{i-1}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta)}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{1}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}}\frac{\mu_{i-1}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{i-1}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta)}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{2}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(5)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=-{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma).

Lemmas
C.3
,
C.5
,
C.7
and the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{n}
give that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty,\,\Delta\to 0,\,n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{\Delta}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{5}\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(l)}\Bigl(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma\Bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle(\beta,\sigma)\in\Theta_{\beta}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
, thus also
\displaystyle T_{3}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0
. The proof is now complete.

C.2.4
Proof of Lemma
B.4

We write
\displaystyle\textstyle{Q(\theta)=\sum_{l=1}^{4}Q_{l}(\theta)}
, where

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{1}\bigl(\theta\bigr)=\tfrac{2}{n\Delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(X_{t_{i}}-\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\gamma,\sigma)-\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigr);

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{2}\bigl(\theta\bigr)=\tfrac{1}{n\Delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\gamma,\sigma)-\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\gamma,\sigma)-\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigr);

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{3}\bigl(\theta\bigr)=\tfrac{2}{n\Delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\gamma,\sigma)-\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigr)^{\top}\Sigma^{-1}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{\Delta}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\gamma,\sigma)\bigr);

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{4}\bigl(\theta\bigr)=-\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Bigl\{\Phi_{2}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)-\Phi_{2}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\beta^{\dagger},\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr\}.

We will show that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty,

\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{1}\bigl(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0;

(C.14)

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{2}\bigl(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\int\bigl(e(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)-e(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(e(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)-e(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\bigr)\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

(C.15)

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{3}\bigl(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\bigl(e(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)-e(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(e(x;\theta^{\dagger})-e(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\bigr)\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

(C.16)

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{4}\bigl(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},i_{2},j_{1},j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}\int\Bigl\{{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)-{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\Bigr\}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigl\{{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma^{\dagger})-{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\Bigr\}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

(C.17)

uniformly in
\displaystyle(\beta,\sigma)\in\Theta_{\beta}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
, where for each
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
,
\displaystyle e(\cdot;\theta):\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
is given by

\displaystyle\displaystyle e(x;\theta)=\big[\,V_{R,0}(x,\beta)^{\top},\,\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{V}_{0}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)^{\top}\,\big]^{\top},\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{d};

recall here that the
\displaystyle d\times d
matrix
\displaystyle G=G(x;\theta)
is defined in (
A.6
). Upon showing the above convergences, the proof of Lemma 5.4 will be complete since we have
\displaystyle Q_{3}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\hat{\sigma}_{n})\rightarrow 0
,
\displaystyle Q_{4}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\hat{\sigma}_{n})\rightarrow 0
, in probability under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}
, due to the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\sigma}_{n}
and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}(x;\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)-{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)=0,

for any
\displaystyle x\in\mathbb{R}^{d},\;(\gamma,\sigma)\in\Theta_{\gamma}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
.

Proof of (
C.14
) and (
C.15
).

From Lemma
C.5
, we immediately have that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,
\displaystyle Q_{1}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\rightarrow 0
, in probability under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}
.
Thus, Lemma
C.3
and the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{n}
yield the convergence (
C.15
).

Proof of (
C.16
).

We have
\displaystyle Q_{3}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=Q_{3,1}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)+Q_{3,2}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)
, where

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{3,1}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=\tfrac{2}{n\sqrt{\Delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq d\\
d_{R}+1\leq j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\times\bigl(e_{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)-e_{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\bigr)\frac{\bigl(V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n})\bigr)}{\sqrt{\Delta}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{3,2}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\times\bigl(e_{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)-e_{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\bigr)\bigl(e_{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta^{\dagger})-e_{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\bigr).

Notice that

\displaystyle\displaystyle e({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta^{\dagger},\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)-e({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=\begin{bmatrix}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta^{\dagger})-V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\\[5.69046pt]
\tfrac{1}{2}\partial_{x_{R}}^{\top}V_{S,0}(x,\hat{\gamma}_{n})\bigl(V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta^{\dagger})-V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)\end{bmatrix}.

Hence,
\displaystyle Q_{3,1}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=0
from Lemma
C.1
.
Furthermore, from Lemma
C.3
, we directly have

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{3,2}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\bigl(e(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)-e(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\bigl(e(x;\theta^{\dagger})-e(x;\beta^{\dagger},\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\bigr)\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx).

We have now obtained the convergence (
C.16
).

Proof of (
C.17
).

Recall
\displaystyle\textstyle{\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{l=1}^{5}\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(l)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)}
, see Section
C.2.3
. We have

\displaystyle\textstyle{-\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Phi_{2}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=\sum_{k=1}^{5}Q_{4,k}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)}
,
where

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{4,k}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)=-\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(k)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma).

We will check the convergence of each term
\displaystyle Q_{4,k}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)
. From Lemmas
C.3
,
C.5
and the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{n}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{4,1}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}-2\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\int{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma^{\dagger})\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{4,2}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\ \ \ Q_{4,3}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\ \ \ Q_{4,5}((\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma))\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\mathrm{tr}\left({G}(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\right)\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx).

For term
\displaystyle Q_{4,4}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle Q_{4,4}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},i_{2}\leq d\\
d_{R}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}\Biggl\{{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\times\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n})}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\frac{V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n})}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},i_{2}\leq d,\\
1\leq j_{1}\leq d_{R}\\
d_{R}+1\leq j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}\Biggl\{{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigl({V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)}\bigr)\bigl(V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n})\bigr)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad-\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq i_{1},i_{2}\leq d\\
d_{R}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}\Biggl\{{G}_{i_{1},i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\times\bigl({\hat{V}_{0}V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})-\hat{V}_{0}V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma))}\bigr)\bigl(V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\hat{\gamma}_{n})\bigr)\Biggr\}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)).

for
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\in\mathscr{S}
.
Due to Lemmas
C.3
,
C.7
and the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\gamma}_{n}
, we immediately have that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty,\;\Delta\to 0,\;n\Delta\to\infty
,
\displaystyle Q_{4,4}(\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\rightarrow 0
, in probability under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}
, uniformly in
\displaystyle(\beta,\sigma)\in\Theta_{\beta}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
. Finally, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle-\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Phi_{2}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\beta,\hat{\gamma}_{n},\sigma)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle-2\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\int{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\beta,\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)

(C.18)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl\{{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}j_{1}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}j_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma){\Sigma}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma^{\dagger})-{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(x;\gamma^{\dagger},\sigma)\Bigr\}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

uniformly in
\displaystyle(\beta,\sigma)\in\Theta_{\beta}\times\Theta_{\sigma}
. From (
C.18
), we obtain the convergence (
C.17
).

C.2.5
Proof of Lemma
C.7

We will prove only convergence (
C.13
) since (
C.12
) is deduced in a similar manner. Define for
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
and
\displaystyle d_{R}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\zeta_{\lambda}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle:=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggl\{f({X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma^{\dagger}))}{\sqrt{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\frac{V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma^{\dagger}))}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\biggr\}.

It holds that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty,\;\Delta\to 0,\;n\Delta\to\infty
, then

\displaystyle\displaystyle|\zeta_{\lambda}(\theta)|\leq{\lambda^{2}}\left|\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta}}(\hat{\gamma}_{n}-\gamma^{\dagger})\right|^{2}\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|f({X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)B^{j_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}})B^{j_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}})|\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta,\;\lambda\in(0,1]
, where we used the condition (
H6
), Lemma
C.3
and the convergence (
B.1
) in the main text.
Now, we obtain
\displaystyle\zeta_{\lambda}(\theta)\rightarrow 0
, in probability under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}
, uniformly in
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
and the proof is complete.

C.3
Proof of Technical Results for Theorem
4.2

C.3.1
Proof of Lemma
B.5

From the definition of the contrast
\displaystyle\ell_{n,\Delta}(\theta),\;\theta\in\Theta
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle-M_{n,\Delta}^{jj}\partial_{\theta_{j}}\ell_{n,\Delta}(\theta)=-M_{n,\Delta}^{jj}\sum_{i=1}^{n}L_{i}^{j}(\theta),

for
\displaystyle 1\leq j\leq d_{\theta}
, where
\displaystyle L_{i}^{j}(\theta)
is explicitly given later. Let
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},j_{2},j_{3},j_{4}\leq d_{\theta}
. Then, we will show the following results:

a.

As
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
and
\displaystyle n\Delta^{3}\to 0
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{1}j_{1}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0.

(C.19)

b.

As
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{1}j_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{2}j_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}4I_{j_{1}j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger});

(C.20)

\displaystyle\displaystyle M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{1}j_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{2}j_{2}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{3}j_{3}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{4}j_{4}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{3}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{4}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0.

(C.21)

Upon showing the above results, the proof of Lemma
B.5
is complete due to Theorems 3.2 and 3.4 in
Hall and Heyde 2014
. We consider the partial derivatives of

\ell_{n,\Delta}(\theta^{\dagger})=\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})+\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta^{\dagger}),

where

\displaystyle\displaystyle\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(X_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)+\sum_{i=1}^{n}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)|;

(C.22)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta)=-2\Delta\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Phi_{2}(\Delta,X_{t_{i-1}},X_{t_{i}};\theta).

(C.23)

We have for
\displaystyle 1\leq j\leq d_{\theta}
:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial_{\theta_{j}}\ell_{n,\Delta}(\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\Bigl\{\partial_{\theta_{j}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)+\partial_{\theta_{j}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta)\Bigr\}\Big|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl\{L_{i}^{j,(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})+L_{i}^{j,(2)}(\theta^{\dagger})\bigr\},

where we have set, for
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1}\leq d_{\beta},\;d_{\beta}+1\leq j_{2}\leq d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma},\;d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq j_{3}\leq d_{\theta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle L_{i}^{j_{1},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})=-2\sqrt{\Delta}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}\mu_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\beta)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)\bigr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}};

(C.24)

\displaystyle\displaystyle L_{i}^{j_{2},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})=\biggl\{-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}d_{R}+1\leq i_{1}\leq d\\
1\leq i_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\frac{\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{0}^{i_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\,\nu_{i}^{i_{2}}(\Delta;\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad-\sqrt{\Delta}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}d_{R}+1\leq i_{1}\leq d\\
1\leq i_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\hat{V}_{0}V_{0}^{i_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\,\nu_{i}^{i_{2}}(\Delta;\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)^{\top}\partial_{{\theta}_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\,\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)+\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)|\biggr\}\Bigr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}};

(C.25)

\displaystyle\displaystyle L_{i}^{j_{3},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})=\biggl\{\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)+\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad\quad-2\sqrt{\Delta}\,\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}\mu_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\,\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta)\biggr\}\Bigr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}},

(C.26)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle L_{i}^{j,(2)}(\theta^{\dagger})=-2\Delta\times\partial_{\theta_{j}}\Phi_{2}(\Delta,X_{t_{i-1}},X_{t_{i}};\theta)\bigr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}},\ \ 1\leq j\leq d_{\theta}.

(C.27)

Proof of (
C.19
).
Using Lemmas
C.4
,
C.6
, we have for
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1}\leq d_{\beta},\;d_{\beta}+1\leq j_{2}\leq d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=\mathcal{E}^{j_{1},(1)}(\Delta^{2},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1},(2)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=\mathcal{E}^{j_{1},(2)}(\Delta^{3},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{2},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=\mathcal{E}^{{j_{2},(1)}}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{2},(2)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=\mathcal{E}^{{j_{2},(2)}}(\Delta^{2},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger}),

for some
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{{j_{i_{1}},(i_{2})}}\in\mathscr{S}
,
\displaystyle i_{1},i_{2}=1,2
. Notice that in the above computation, we used (
A.2
) and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)|

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\mathrm{tr}\Bigl({\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=-\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(X_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr).

(C.28)

It follows from Lemmas
C.3
,
C.5
that
as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
and
\displaystyle n\Delta^{3}\to 0
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n\Delta}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1},(1)}+L_{i}^{j_{1},(2)}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\;0;

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{\sqrt{\Delta}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{2},(1)}+L_{i}^{j_{2},(2)}|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\;0.

Now, let
\displaystyle d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq j_{3}\leq d_{\theta}
.
Lemmas
C.4
,
C.6
give

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{3},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=\Bigl\{\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(X_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}_{1}(X_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\bigr)+\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\Delta\,\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\Xi({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\bigr)\Bigr\}\Bigr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}+\mathcal{E}^{j_{3},(1)}(\Delta^{2},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad=2\Delta\mathrm{tr}\bigl(G({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\bigr)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}+\mathcal{E}^{j_{3},(1)}(\Delta^{2},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger}),

(C.29)

for
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j_{3},(1)}\in\mathscr{S}
, where we used (
C.28
). Furthermore, we have from Lemma
C.6

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{3},(2)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=-2\Delta\times\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}\Phi_{2}(\Delta,X_{t_{i-1}},X_{t_{i}};\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad=-2\Delta\mathrm{tr}\,\bigl(G({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{j_{3}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\bigr)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}+\mathcal{E}^{j_{3},(2)}(\Delta^{2},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger}),

(C.30)

for
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{j_{3},(2)}\in\mathscr{S}
. Hence, it follows from (
C.29
) a (
C.3.1
) that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
and
\displaystyle n\Delta^{4}\to 0
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{3},(1)}(\theta^{\dagger})+L_{i}^{j_{3},(2)}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\sqrt{n\Delta^{4}},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0.

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\in\mathscr{S}
.

Proof of (
C.20
).

We check (
C.20
) for the following two cases:
(a)
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d_{\beta}
;
(b)
\displaystyle d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d_{\theta}
,
since the proof for other cases are identical. Using (
C.24
), (
C.27
) and Lemmas
C.1
,
C.3
,
C.5
, we obtain the following convergence for
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d_{\beta}
:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n\Delta}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]

(C.31)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}=1}^{d}\Bigl\{{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{3}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}\mu_{1}^{i_{1}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta){\Sigma}_{1,i_{3}i_{4}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\times{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{2}i_{4}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\mu_{1}^{i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\Bigr\}\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\,+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}\mu_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\mu_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Sigma_{1,RR}^{-1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}4I_{j_{1}j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger}),

as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
, for
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\in\mathscr{S}
. In the third equation of (
C.31
), we have applied Lemma
C.1
to obtain:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}\mu_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\mu_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,RR}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\;+\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,RS}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{x_{R}}^{\top}V_{S,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\gamma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,RR}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\Sigma_{1,RR}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\sigma)\Sigma_{1,RR}^{-1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\;+\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,RS}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\Sigma_{1,RS}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\Sigma_{1,RR}^{-1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{4}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Sigma_{1,RR}^{-1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}V_{R,0}({X}_{t_{i-1}},\beta).

We next consider case (b),
\displaystyle d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d_{\theta}
.
(
C.26
) and (
C.27
) yield – we omit the argument
\displaystyle({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)
from the elements of the
\displaystyle\Sigma
matrix below, and instead apply the substitution
\displaystyle({X}_{t_{i-1}};\gamma,\sigma)\leftrightarrow(i)
to reduce the size of the expressions:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\Biggl\{\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i)\,\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{3}i_{4}}(i)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\times\Bigl({\Sigma}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{3}i_{4}}(i)+{\Sigma}_{1,i_{1}i_{3}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{2}i_{4}}(i)+{\Sigma}_{1,i_{1}i_{4}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{2}i_{3}}(i)\Bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(i)|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i)\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(i)|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(i)|\;\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(i)|\Biggr\}\Biggr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\biggr\{\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{3}i_{4}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{1}i_{3}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{2}i_{4}}(i)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\sum_{i_{1},i_{2},i_{3},i_{4}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{3}i_{4}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{1}i_{4}}(i){\Sigma}_{1,i_{2}i_{3}}(i)\biggr\}\biggr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger}),

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\tfrac{2}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{j_{1}}}{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{j_{2}}}{\Sigma}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\Bigr)\Bigr|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger}),

for
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\in\mathscr{S}
,
where in the second equality we used (
C.28
) and in the last equality we used:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial_{\theta_{j}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}i_{2}}(i)=-\sum_{l_{1},l_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,i_{1}l_{1}}(i)\partial_{\theta_{j}}{\Sigma}_{1,l_{1}l_{2}}(i){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,l_{2}i_{2}}(i)

Finally, Lemma
C.3
yields that as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}\bigl[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}\bigr]\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}4I_{j_{1}j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger}),\qquad d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d_{\theta}.

Proof of (
C.21
).

From (
C.24
), (
C.25
), (
C.26
) and (
C.27
), we deduce that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{1}j_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{2}j_{2}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{3}j_{3}}M_{n,\Delta}^{j_{4}j_{4}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\theta^{\dagger}}[L_{i}^{j_{1}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{2}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{3}}(\theta^{\dagger})L_{i}^{j_{4}}(\theta^{\dagger})|\mathcal{F}_{t_{i-1}}]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad=\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathcal{E}(1,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta^{\dagger})\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

(C.32)

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}\in\mathscr{S}
.

C.3.2
Proof of Lemma
B.6

Recall
\displaystyle\textstyle{\ell_{n,\Delta}(\theta)=\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)+\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta)}
, where the definitions of
\displaystyle\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(i)}(\theta)
,
\displaystyle i=1,2
, are given in (
C.22
) and (
C.23
). We divide the function
\displaystyle B_{n,\Delta}(\theta)
,
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
, into two parts:

\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{n,\Delta}(\theta)=M_{n,\Delta}\,\partial_{\theta}^{2}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)\,M_{n,\Delta}+M_{n,\Delta}\,\partial_{\theta}^{2}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta)\,M_{n,\Delta}\equiv B_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)+B_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta),

where we recall
\displaystyle M_{n,\Delta}=\mathrm{diag}(v_{n,\Delta})
with

\displaystyle\displaystyle v_{n,\Delta}=\Bigl[\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n\Delta}},\ldots,\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n\Delta}}}_{d_{\beta}-\mathrm{dim}},\underbrace{\sqrt{\tfrac{\Delta}{n}},\ldots,\sqrt{\tfrac{\Delta}{n}}}_{d_{\gamma}-\mathrm{dim}},\underbrace{\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}},\ldots,\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{n}}}_{d_{\sigma}-\mathrm{dim}}\Bigr]^{\top},\qquad d_{\theta}=d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+d_{\sigma}.

We will show that, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty
,
\displaystyle\Delta\to 0
and
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2I(\theta^{\dagger});

(C.33)

\displaystyle\displaystyle B_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}\bigl(\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

(C.34)

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in(0,1]
.

Proof of (
C.33
).

We will compute the second derivatives of
\displaystyle\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)
,
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
.
Let
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq d_{\beta},\;d_{\beta}+1\leq k_{3},k_{4}\leq d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma},\;d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq k_{5},k_{6}\leq d_{\theta}
. Using the expression of (
C.11
) and Lemma
C.1
, we have:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n\Delta}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,\biggl\{2\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad-\tfrac{2}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad-2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq d_{R}\\
1\leq j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\bigl(V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\beta^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\beta)\bigr)\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\mu^{j_{2}}_{1}(x;\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{\Delta}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,\biggl\{2\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+{\Delta}\,\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\bigl(V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\theta)\,\bigl(V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=d_{R}+1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{3},k_{4})}(1,x,\theta)\bigl(V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma)\bigr)+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{3},k_{4})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,\biggl\{\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)|+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\,\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{5},k_{6})}(1,x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\tfrac{1}{\Delta}\bigl(V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\bigl(V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad-\bigl({V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)}\bigr)^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\hat{V}_{0}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad-\bigl({V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)}\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\hat{V}_{0}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{5},k_{6})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,\biggl\{{2}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq d\\
d_{R}+1\leq j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu_{1}^{j_{1}}(x;\theta)\Sigma^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{3}}V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+{2}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu^{j_{1}}_{1}(x;\theta)\,\frac{\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta)}{\Delta^{\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{2}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+{2}\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\mu^{j_{1}}_{1}(x;\theta)\frac{\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta)}{\Delta^{\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{2}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\nu^{j_{1}}_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{1},k_{3})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n\sqrt{\Delta}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,\biggl\{-2\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu^{j_{1}}_{1}(x;\theta)\frac{\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta)}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{2}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad-2\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\mu^{j_{1}}_{1}(x;\theta)\frac{\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta^{\dagger})-\mu_{\Delta}^{j_{2}}(x;\theta)}{\Delta^{\frac{1}{2}+\mathbf{1}_{\{j_{2}\geq d_{R}+1\}}}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+{\sqrt{\Delta}}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{1},k_{5})}(1,x,\theta)\biggr\}\Bigl|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{\sqrt{\Delta}}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)=\tfrac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\,\biggl\{{\sqrt{\Delta}}\,\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\nu_{i}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\tfrac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\bigl(V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)^{\top}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\bigl(V_{S,0}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=d_{R}+1}^{d}\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{3},k_{5})}(1,x,\theta)+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\,\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{\,(k_{3},k_{5})}(1,x,\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+{\mathcal{E}}^{(k_{3},k_{5})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}},

for
\displaystyle\theta=(\beta,\gamma,\sigma)\in\Theta
, with
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(m_{1},m_{2})},\mathcal{E}^{(m_{1},m_{2})}_{j_{1}j_{2}},\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{\,(m_{1},m_{2})}_{j_{1}j_{2}}\in\mathscr{S}
, for all
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d,\,1\leq m_{1},m_{2}\leq d_{\theta}
.
Due to Lemmas
C.1
,
C.3
,
C.5
,
C.7
and the consistency of the estimator
\displaystyle\hat{\theta}_{n}
, we obtain the following convergences. As
\displaystyle n\to\infty,\;\Delta\to 0
,
\displaystyle n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n\Delta}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)\bigr)^{\top}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\mu_{1}(x;\theta)\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle=2\int\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}V_{R,0}(x,\beta)\bigr)^{\top}\Sigma_{1,RR}^{-1}(x;\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}V_{R,0}(x,\beta)\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{\Delta}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\int\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\bigr)^{\top}\Lambda_{1,SS}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}2\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq j_{1}\leq d\\
d_{R}+1\leq j_{2}\leq d\end{subarray}}\int\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mu_{1}^{j_{1}}(x;\theta){\Sigma}^{-1}_{1,j_{1}j_{2}}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma)\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)=0;

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl\{\tfrac{1}{n\sqrt{\Delta}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\tfrac{\sqrt{\Delta}}{n}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)\Bigr\}|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0;

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(1)}(\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}\int\Bigl\{\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr)+\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)|\Bigr\}\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad\quad=\int\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr)\Bigl|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}}\,\nu_{\theta^{\dagger}}(dx),

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in[0,1]
, where we used

\displaystyle\displaystyle\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\log|{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)|=-\mathrm{tr}\bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma){\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\bigr)-\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}_{1}^{-1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr)

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}_{1}^{-1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr)=-\mathrm{tr}\Bigl(\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}{\Sigma}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\,{\Sigma}^{-1}_{1}(x;\gamma,\sigma)\Bigr).

Hence, we obtain the convergence (
C.33
).

Proof of (
C.34
).

Let
\displaystyle 1\leq l_{1},l_{2}\leq d_{\theta}
. We have
\displaystyle\textstyle{M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{1}l_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{2}l_{2}}\partial_{\theta_{l_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{l_{2}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta)=\sum_{l=1}^{4}\Upsilon_{l}(\theta)}
, where we have set:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{1}(\theta)=-2\Delta M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{1}l_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{2}l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{l_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{l_{2}}}{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{2}(\theta)=-2\Delta M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{1}l_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{2}l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{l_{1}}}{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{l_{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{3}(\theta)=-2\Delta M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{1}l_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{2}l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}\partial_{\theta_{l_{2}}}{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{l_{1}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{4}(\theta)=-2\Delta M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{1}l_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{2}l_{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{d}{G}_{i_{1}i_{2}}({X}_{t_{i-1}};\theta)\partial_{\theta_{l_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{l_{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta).

Notice that each term of
\displaystyle\Upsilon_{l}(\theta)
has a factor
\displaystyle\Delta
in the front. We obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{1}\bigl(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\lambda(\theta^{\dagger}-\hat{\theta}_{n})\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in(0,1]
from the same discussion we have shown the convergence (
C.18
). We turn to terms
\displaystyle\Upsilon_{2}\bigl(\theta\bigr)
and
\displaystyle\Upsilon_{3}\bigl(\theta\bigr)
. Recall
\displaystyle\textstyle{\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{l=1}^{5}\eta_{(i_{1},i_{2})}^{(l)}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)}
in Section
C.2.3
. For
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1}\leq d_{\beta}
,
\displaystyle d_{\beta}+1\leq k_{2}\leq d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}
,
\displaystyle d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq k_{3}\leq d_{\theta}
, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{1})}(\sqrt{\Delta^{3}},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta),

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\biggl\{\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{2})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{2})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j}^{\,(k_{2})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{\,(k_{2})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\frac{V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{2})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\biggr\}\Bigr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\biggl\{\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{3})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=d_{R}+1}^{d}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{\,(k_{3})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}\frac{V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}

\displaystyle\displaystyle+\sum_{j=d_{R}+1}^{d}{\mathcal{E}}_{j}^{(k_{3})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j}^{\,(k_{3})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{3})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\biggr\}\Bigr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}},

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(m)},\,\mathcal{E}^{(m)}_{j_{1}},\,\mathcal{E}^{(m)}_{j_{1}j_{2}},\,\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{\,(m)}_{j_{1}},\,\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}^{(m)}_{j_{1}j_{2}}\in\mathscr{S}
, for
\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d
and
\displaystyle 1\leq m\leq d_{\theta}
. Applying Lemmas
C.3
,
C.5
and
C.7
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{k}\bigl(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\lambda(\theta^{\dagger}-\hat{\theta}_{n})\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,\qquad k=2,3,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in(0,1]
.
Finally, we consider the term
\displaystyle\Upsilon_{4}(\theta)
. We compute the second order partial derivatives of
\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)
with respect to the parameter
\displaystyle\theta\in\Theta
as follows. For
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2}\leq d_{\beta},\;d_{\beta}+1\leq k_{3},k_{4}\leq d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma},\;d_{\beta}+d_{\gamma}+1\leq k_{5},k_{6}\leq d_{\theta}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{2}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\biggl\{\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{1},k_{2})}(\sqrt{\Delta^{3}},x,\theta)\,\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=d_{R}+1}^{d}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j}^{(k_{1},k_{2})}(\sqrt{\Delta^{3}},x,\theta)\frac{V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j}(x,\gamma)}{\sqrt{\Delta}}+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{1},k_{2})}(\Delta^{2},x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{4}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\biggl\{\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{3},k_{4})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{3},k_{4})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{3},k_{4})}(1,x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{6}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\biggl\{\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{5},k_{6})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=d_{R}+1}^{d}\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{\,(k_{5},k_{6})}(1,x,\theta)\bigl(V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{1}}(x,\gamma)\bigr)\bigl(V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma^{\dagger})-V_{0}^{j_{2}}(x,\gamma)\bigr)

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{5},k_{6})}(\sqrt{\Delta},x,\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{5},k_{6})}(\Delta,x,\theta)\biggr\}\biggr|_{x={X}_{t_{i-1}}};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{1},k_{3})}(\sqrt{\Delta^{3}},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{1},k_{3})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{1},k_{5})}(\sqrt{\Delta^{3}},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{1},k_{5})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Delta\,\partial_{\theta_{k_{3}}}\partial_{\theta_{k_{5}}}\mathcal{H}_{(i_{1},i_{2})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},{X}_{t_{i}};\theta)=\sum_{j_{1},j_{2}=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(k_{3},k_{5})}(\Delta,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\nu_{i}^{j_{1}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})\nu_{i}^{j_{2}}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\mathcal{E}_{j}^{(k_{3},k_{5})}(\sqrt{\Delta},{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta)\nu_{i}^{j}(\Delta;\theta^{\dagger})+\mathcal{E}^{(k_{3},k_{5})}(1,{X}_{t_{i-1}},\theta),

where
\displaystyle\mathcal{E}^{(m_{1},m_{2})},\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}}^{(m_{1},m_{2})},\mathcal{E}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{(m_{1},m_{2})},\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j_{1}}^{\,(m_{1},m_{2})},\widetilde{\mathcal{E}}_{j_{1}j_{2}}^{\,(m_{1},m_{2})}\in\mathscr{S}
for all

\displaystyle 1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d
and all
\displaystyle 1\leq m_{1},m_{2}\leq d_{\theta}
.
Hence, from Lemmas
C.3
,
C.5
and the consistency of
\displaystyle\hat{\theta}_{n}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Upsilon_{4}\bigl(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\lambda(\theta^{\dagger}-\hat{\theta}_{n})\bigr)\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in(0,1]
. Thus, as
\displaystyle n\to\infty,\;\Delta\to 0,\;n\Delta\to\infty
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{1}l_{1}}M_{n,\Delta}^{l_{2}l_{2}}\partial_{\theta_{l_{1}}}\partial_{\theta_{l_{2}}}\ell_{n,\Delta}^{(2)}(\theta)|_{\theta=\theta^{\dagger}+\lambda(\hat{\theta}_{n}-\theta^{\dagger})}\xrightarrow{\mathbb{P}_{\theta^{\dagger}}}0,

uniformly in
\displaystyle\lambda\in(0,1]
, which implies the convergence (
C.34
).

Appendix D
Proof of Proposition
2.1

We define

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x}:=Z_{\Delta}^{x}+\bar{Z}_{\Delta}^{x}
,
with

\displaystyle{Z}_{\Delta}^{x}=[Z_{R,\Delta}^{\top},Z_{S,\Delta}^{\top}]^{\top}

and

\displaystyle\bar{Z}_{\Delta}^{x}=[\mathbf{0}_{d_{R}}^{\top},\bar{Z}_{S,\Delta}^{\top}]^{\top}

given as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle Z_{R,\Delta}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=x_{R}+V_{R,0}(x,\beta)\Delta+\sum_{k=1}^{d_{R}}V_{R,k}(x,\sigma)B_{k,\Delta}+\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=0}^{d_{R}}\hat{V}_{k_{1}}V_{R,k_{2}}(x,\theta)\zeta_{k_{1}k_{2},\Delta};

\displaystyle\displaystyle Z_{S,\Delta}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=x_{S}+V_{S,0}(x,\theta)\Delta+\sum_{k=0}^{d_{R}}\hat{V}_{k}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)\zeta_{k0,\Delta};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\bar{Z}_{S,\Delta}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\sum_{\alpha\in\{0,\ldots,d_{R}\}^{2}\setminus\{0,0\}}\hat{V}_{\alpha_{1}}\hat{V}_{\alpha_{2}}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)\eta_{\alpha_{1}\alpha_{2},\Delta}.

We are able to show that
\displaystyle Z_{\Delta}^{x}
has a third order of accuracy on a single step in the sense of weak approximation for
\displaystyle X_{\Delta}^{x}
. In fact, one can check that random variables
\displaystyle\zeta_{k_{1}k_{2},\Delta},\;k_{1},k_{2}=1,\ldots,d_{R}
satisfy the moment conditions given in
Lemma 2.1.5 in
Milstein and Tretyakov 2021
and then, the scheme has local weak third order precision from Theorem 2.1.6 in
Milstein and Tretyakov 2021
. Hence, it suffices to show that for
\displaystyle\varphi\in C_{p}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
, there exist constants
\displaystyle C,q>0
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi(\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x})]-\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi({Z}_{\Delta}^{x})]\Bigr|\leq C(1+|x|^{q})\Delta^{3}.

(D.1)

This is because once we have the bound (
D.1
), we obtain:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi({X}_{\Delta}^{x})]-\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi(\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x})]\Bigr|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi({X}_{\Delta}^{x})]-\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi({Z}_{\Delta}^{x})]\Bigr|+\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi(\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x})]-\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi({Z}_{\Delta}^{x})]\Bigr|

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq C(1+|x|^{q})\Delta^{3}.

Now, we define

\displaystyle{\Delta}_{\bar{X}}=\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x}-x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}

and

\displaystyle{\Delta}_{Z}:={Z}_{\Delta}^{x}-x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Let
\displaystyle\varphi\in C_{p}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
. We consider the Taylor expansions of
\displaystyle\varphi(\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x})
and
\displaystyle\varphi(Z_{\Delta}^{x})
around
\displaystyle\varphi(x)
, respectively as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varphi(\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x})=\varphi(x)+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\sum_{\beta\in\{1,\ldots,d\}^{k}}\frac{1}{k!}\partial^{\beta}\varphi(x)\prod_{l=1}^{k}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{\bar{X}}+{R}^{\varphi}_{\bar{X}}(x,\Delta;\theta);

\displaystyle\displaystyle\varphi(Z_{\Delta}^{x})=\varphi(x)+\sum_{k=1}^{5}\sum_{\beta\in\{1,\ldots,d\}^{k}}\frac{1}{k!}\partial^{\beta}\varphi(x)\prod_{l=1}^{k}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{Z}+{R}^{\varphi}_{Z}(x,\Delta;\theta),

where
\displaystyle{R}^{\varphi}_{\bar{X}}(x,\Delta;\theta)
,
\displaystyle{R}^{\varphi}_{Z}(x,\Delta;\theta)
are remainder terms specified as follows. Under condition (
H2
), there exist constants
\displaystyle C,q>0
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\bigl[R^{\varphi}_{\bar{X}}(x,\Delta;\theta)\bigr]|+|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[R^{\varphi}_{Z}(x,\Delta;\theta)]|\leq C(1+|x|^{q})\Delta^{3},

that comes from the polynomial growth of derivatives of
\displaystyle\varphi
with estimates given as follows. For any
\displaystyle p\geq 1
, there exist constants
\displaystyle C,q>0
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[|\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x}|^{p}]\leq C(1+|x|^{q})

and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\Bigl[\prod_{l=1}^{6}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{\bar{X}}\Bigr]\Bigr|=\mathcal{O}(\Delta^{3}),\qquad\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\Bigl[\prod_{l=1}^{6}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{Z}\Bigr]\Bigr|=\mathcal{O}(\Delta^{3}),

for any multi-index
\displaystyle\beta\in\{1,\ldots,d\}^{6}
.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi(\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{x})]-\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\varphi(Z_{\Delta}^{x})]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{5}\sum_{\beta\in\{1,\ldots,d\}^{k}}\frac{1}{k!}\partial^{\beta}\varphi(x)\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\Bigl[\prod_{l=1}^{k}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{\bar{X}}-\prod_{l=1}^{k}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{Z}\Bigr]

\displaystyle\displaystyle\qquad+{R}^{\varphi}_{\bar{X}}(x,\Delta;\theta)-{R}^{\varphi}_{Z}(x,\Delta;\theta).

It follows that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\Bigl[{\Delta}^{l}_{\bar{X}}-{\Delta}^{l}_{Z}\Bigr]=\sum_{l=d_{R}+1}^{d}\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\Bigl[\bar{Z}_{\Delta}^{x,l}\Bigr]=0;

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sum_{\beta\in\{1,\ldots,d\}^{k}}\Bigl|\mathbb{E}_{\theta}\Bigl[\prod_{l=1}^{k}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{\bar{X}}-\prod_{l=1}^{k}{\Delta}^{\beta_{l}}_{Z}\Bigr]\Bigr|\leq c\Delta^{3},\ \ k=2,3,4,5,

for some constant
\displaystyle c>0
from the following properties of the random variable
\displaystyle\eta_{k_{1}k_{2},\Delta}
. For
\displaystyle 1\leq k_{1},k_{2},k_{3}\leq d_{R}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\eta_{k_{1}k_{2},\Delta}]=0,\ \ \mathbb{E}[\eta_{k_{1}k_{2},\Delta}B_{k_{3},\Delta}]=0,\ \ |\mathbb{E}[\eta_{k_{1}k_{2},\Delta}]|=\mathcal{O}(\Delta^{2});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\eta_{k_{1}0,\Delta}]=\mathbb{E}[\eta_{0k_{1},\Delta}]=0,\ \ \bigl|\mathbb{E}[(\eta_{k_{1}0,\Delta}+\eta_{0k_{1},\Delta})B_{k_{3},\Delta}]\bigr|=\mathcal{O}(\Delta^{3}),

Now, we have the bound (
D.1
) and the proof is complete.

Appendix E
Proof of Lemma
3.1

This section provides the small-time expansion for the proposed approximation scheme, given in Lemma
3.1
.
For the derivation, we use Malliavin-Watanabe calculus and consider an asymptotic expansion of the Wiener functional
\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}:\Omega\to\mathbb{R}^{n},\,n\in\mathbb{N}
, that depends on a small parameter
\displaystyle\varepsilon\in(0,1)
. In our analysis, we typically assume
\displaystyle\varepsilon=\sqrt{\Delta}
and then obtain an expansion with respect to
\displaystyle\sqrt{\Delta}
. Section
E
is organised as follows. In Section
E.1
, we introduce notations and quickly review some key results of Malliavin calculus (for more details see e.g.
Nualart 2006
;
Watanabe 1987
). The proof of Lemma
3.1
is given in Section
E.2
.
Proofs of technical results required by Lemma
3.1
are collected in Section
E.3
.

E.1
Preliminaries from Malliavin Calculus

– Wiener space

Let
\displaystyle(\Omega,\mathcal{F},\mathbb{P})
be the
\displaystyle m
-dimensional Wiener space,
\displaystyle m\in\mathbb{N}
, where

\displaystyle\Omega=\{w:[0,\infty)\to\mathbb{R}^{m};\;w(0)=0,w\mathrm{\;is\;continuous}\}
,
\displaystyle\mathcal{F}
is the Borel field over
\displaystyle\Omega
and
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}
the Wiener measure. We denote by
\displaystyle B_{t}=(B_{1,t},\ldots,B_{m,t}),\,t\geq 0
the
\displaystyle m
-dimensional Brownian motion under
\displaystyle\mathbb{P}
.
Let
\displaystyle H=L^{2}([0,\infty);\mathbb{R}^{m})
be a Hilbert space equipped with the inner product
\displaystyle\langle\cdot,\cdot\rangle_{H}
given as:

\langle h_{1},h_{2}\rangle_{H}=\int_{0}^{\infty}h_{1,s}\cdot h_{2,s}\,ds,\qquad h_{1},h_{2}\in H.

We denote by
\displaystyle L^{p}(\Omega;\mathbb{R}^{n})
,
\displaystyle p\in[1,\infty)
,
\displaystyle n\in\mathbb{N}
, the Banach space of Wiener functionals
\displaystyle F:\Omega\to\mathbb{R}^{n}
such that
\displaystyle\|F\|_{p}:=\{\mathbb{E}[|F|^{p}]\}^{1/p}<\infty
, where
\displaystyle|\cdot|
is the Euclidean norm. Similarly,
\displaystyle L^{p}(\Omega;H)
is the set of maps
\displaystyle f:\Omega\to H
such that
\displaystyle\|f\|_{H}\in L^{p}(\Omega;\mathbb{R}):=L^{p}(\Omega)
.

– Malliavin Derivative Operator

For
\displaystyle h\in H
, we define a Wiener integral
\displaystyle W(h)
as
\displaystyle\textstyle{W(h):=\int_{0}^{\infty}h_{s}\cdot dB_{s}}
. We introduce a class of Wiener functionals as:

\mathcal{S}:=\bigl\{F=\varphi\bigl(W(h_{1}),\ldots,W(h_{l})\bigr);\;l\geq 1,\varphi\in C_{p}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{l}),\;h_{1},\ldots,h_{l}\in H\bigr\}.

The Malliavin derivative of
\displaystyle F\in\mathcal{S}
is defined as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle DF=\sum_{i=1}^{l}\partial_{i}\varphi\bigl(W(h_{1}),\ldots,W(h_{l})\bigr)h_{i},\qquad F\in\mathcal{S},

or written as a stochastic process
\displaystyle\{D_{t}F\}_{t\geq 0}
with coordinates specified as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle D_{j,t}F=\sum_{i=1}^{l}\partial_{i}\varphi\bigl(W(h_{1}),\ldots,W(h_{l})\bigr)h_{i,t}^{j},\qquad t\geq 0,\quad 1\leq j\leq m.

For example, one has for
\displaystyle 0<t<T
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle D_{j_{1},t}B_{j_{2},T}=\mathbf{1}_{t\in[0,T],\;j_{1}=j_{2}},\quad D_{j_{1},t}\int_{0}^{T}tdB_{j_{2},t}=t\times\mathbf{1}_{t\in[0,T],\;j_{1}=j_{2}},\qquad 1\leq j_{1},j_{2}\leq d.

The iterated derivative
\displaystyle D^{k}F
is also defined as an
\displaystyle H^{\otimes k}
-valued random variable.
It can be shown that for any
\displaystyle k\in\mathbb{N}
, operator
\displaystyle D^{k}
is closable from
\displaystyle\mathcal{S}
into
\displaystyle L^{p}(\Omega;H^{\otimes k})
for any
\displaystyle\;p>1
. We denote by
\displaystyle\mathbb{D}^{k,p}
,
\displaystyle k\in\mathbb{N},\;p>1
, the completion of
\displaystyle\mathcal{S}
with respect to the norm

\displaystyle\displaystyle\|F\|_{k,p}:=\left\{\mathbb{E}[|F|^{p}]+\sum_{j=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}[\|D^{j}F\|^{p}_{H^{\otimes j}}]\right\}^{1/p}.

We define
\displaystyle\textstyle{\mathbb{D}^{\infty}=\bigcap_{k\in\mathbb{N},\;p>1}}\mathbb{D}^{k,p}
and its dual
\displaystyle\textstyle{\mathbb{D}^{-\infty}=\bigcup_{k\in\mathbb{N},\;p>1}}\mathbb{D}^{-k,p}
, where
\displaystyle\mathbb{D}^{-k,p}
is the dual space of
\displaystyle\mathbb{D}^{k,q}
with
\displaystyle q=p/(p-1)
. The elements in
\displaystyle\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
are referred to as
smooth Wiener functionals in Malliavin sense
. Since the elements in
\displaystyle\mathbb{D}^{-\infty}
are not Wiener functionals in the usual sense, they are called
generalised Wiener functionals
. For
\displaystyle\Phi\in\mathbb{D}^{-\infty},\;G\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
, the coupling is defined as

\displaystyle{}_{-\infty}\langle\Phi,G\rangle_{\infty}:=\mathbb{E}[\,\Phi\,G\,]
.
In particular,
\displaystyle{}_{-\infty}\langle\Phi,1\rangle_{\infty}=\mathbb{E}[\Phi]
is called the
generalised expectation
of
\displaystyle\Phi
.

– Divergence Operator and Duality Formula

The divergence operator
\displaystyle\delta:L^{2}(\Omega;H)\to L^{2}(\Omega)
is the adjoint of
\displaystyle D
characterised by the duality formula:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[F\delta(u)]=\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\int_{0}^{\infty}D_{s}F\cdot u_{s}\,ds\Bigr],\qquad F\in\mathbb{D}^{1,2}.

When
\displaystyle\{u_{t}\}_{t\geq 0}
is a square-integrable stochastic process, adapted to the filtration of Brownian motion,
\displaystyle\delta(u)
coincides with the Itô-integral. In this case, the duality formula is written as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigl[F\int_{0}^{\infty}u_{s}\cdot dB_{s}\Bigr]=\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\int_{0}^{\infty}D_{s}F\cdot u_{s}\,ds\Bigr].

(E.1)

In particular, for

\displaystyle F=\varphi(G)
,
\displaystyle\varphi\in C_{b}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
, where

\displaystyle G=(G^{1},\ldots,G^{N})
with
\displaystyle G^{i}\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
, formula (
E.1
) is interpreted as

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\varphi(G)\int_{0}^{\infty}u_{s}\cdot dB_{s}\Bigr]=\sum_{i=1}^{N}\mathbb{E}\Bigl[\partial_{i}\varphi(G)\int_{0}^{\infty}D_{s}G^{i}\cdot u_{s}\,ds\Bigr],

(E.2)

where we applied the chain rule of the Malliavin derivative.

– Non-degeneracy of Wiener Functional and Malliavin Integration by Parts

For a Wiener functional
\displaystyle F=(F^{1},\ldots,F^{N})
with
\displaystyle F^{i}\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
,
\displaystyle 1\leq i\leq N
, the Malliavin covariance is defined as an
\displaystyle N\times N
non-negative definite matrix with elements
\displaystyle\sigma^{F}_{ij}=\langle DF^{i},DF^{j}\rangle_{H}
,
\displaystyle 1\leq i,j\leq N
. When the matrix
\displaystyle\sigma^{F}
is invertible a.s. and
\displaystyle\|(\det\sigma^{F})^{-1}\|_{p}<\infty
for every
\displaystyle 1\leq p<\infty
,
\displaystyle F
is said to be
non-degenerate in Malliavin sense
(henceforth, we simply say non-degenerate).
Now, we introduce the integration by parts on Wiener space that plays an important role in the proof of Lemma
3.1
and Theorem
4.3
. Let
\displaystyle G\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
,
\displaystyle f\in C_{b}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
and
\displaystyle F\in(\mathbb{D}^{\infty})^{N}
be non-degenerate, with the inverse of Malliavin covariance denoted by
\displaystyle\gamma^{F}\equiv(\sigma^{F})^{-1}
. Then, for any multi-index
\displaystyle\alpha\in\{1,\ldots,N\}^{k}
,
\displaystyle k\in\mathbb{N}
, there exists
\displaystyle H_{\alpha}\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\partial^{\alpha}f(F)G]=\mathbb{E}[f(F)H_{\alpha}(F,G)],

where
\displaystyle H_{\alpha}
is recursively defined as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle H_{(i)}(F,G)=\sum_{j=1}^{N}\delta\bigl(\gamma^{F}_{ij}\,G\,DF^{j}\bigr),\ 1\leq i\leq N,\ \ \ H_{\alpha}(F,G)=H_{(\alpha_{k})}\bigl(F,H_{(\alpha_{1},\ldots,\alpha_{k-1})}(F,G)\bigr).

In the proof of Lemma
H.1
, the following estimate on the stochastic weight
\displaystyle H_{\alpha}(F,G)
(see e.g. p.102 in
Nualart 2006
) is frequently used. For
\displaystyle\alpha\in\{1,\ldots,N\}^{k}
,
\displaystyle k\geq 1
and
\displaystyle 1\leq p<q<\infty
, there exist constants
\displaystyle c_{1},c_{2},\beta,\gamma,\lambda
and integers
\displaystyle m,n
such that

\displaystyle\displaystyle\left\|H_{\alpha}(F,G)\right\|_{p}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq c_{1}\,\|\gamma^{F}DF\|_{k,\beta}^{k}\,\|G\|_{k,q}

\displaystyle\displaystyle\leq c_{2}\,\|(\det\sigma^{F})^{-1}\|_{\gamma}^{m}\,\|DF\|_{k,\lambda}^{n}\,\|G\|_{k,q}.

(E.3)

– Generalised Expectation

Let
\displaystyle\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
be the space of real tempered distributions on
\displaystyle\mathbb{R}^{N}
. For
\displaystyle T\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
and a non-degenerate
\displaystyle F\in(\mathbb{D}^{\infty})^{N}
, the composition
\displaystyle T\circ F=T(F)
is well-defined as an element of
\displaystyle\mathbb{D}^{-\infty}
, and the generalised expectation is defined as
\displaystyle{}_{-\infty}\langle T(F),1\rangle_{\infty}=\mathbb{E}\bigl[T(F)\bigr].

In particular, for the Dirac measure
\displaystyle\textstyle{\delta_{y}}
at
\displaystyle\textstyle{y\in\mathbb{R}^{N}}
(
\displaystyle\textstyle{\delta_{y}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{N})}
) and a non-degenerate Wiener functional
\displaystyle F\in(\mathbb{D}^{\infty})^{N}
,
\displaystyle\delta_{y}(F)\in\mathbb{D}^{-\infty}
is well-defined and the probability law of
\displaystyle F
has a smooth density
\displaystyle p^{F}(y)
with respect to the Lebesgue measure
\displaystyle dy
on
\displaystyle\mathbb{R}^{N}
. Then, it can be shown that the density is given as the following generalised expectation:

\displaystyle\displaystyle p^{F}(y)={}_{-\infty}\langle\delta_{y}(F),1\rangle_{\infty}=\mathbb{E}[\,\delta_{y}(F)\,].

Also, it holds that for any
\displaystyle f\in C_{b}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
,
\displaystyle G\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
and non-degenerate Wiener functional
\displaystyle F\in(\mathbb{D}^{\infty})^{N}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[f(F)G]

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}f(y)\,p^{F}(y)\,\mathbb{E}[G|F=y]dy

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}f(y){}_{-\infty}\langle\delta_{y}(F),G\rangle_{\infty}dy=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{N}}f(y)\,\mathbb{E}[\,\delta_{y}(F)G\,]\,dy,

(E.4)

that leads to

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\,\delta_{y}(F)G\,]=p^{F}(y)\cdot\mathbb{E}[\,G|F=y\,],

(E.5)

for almost all
\displaystyle y
such that
\displaystyle p^{F}(y)>0
.

– Asymptotic Expansion of Wiener Functional.

Assume that a Wiener functional
\displaystyle F
depends on a small parameter
\displaystyle\varepsilon\in(0,1)
,

i.e.
\displaystyle F=F^{\varepsilon}=(F^{\varepsilon,1},\ldots,F^{\varepsilon,N})
. We consider the composition of a tempered distribution
\displaystyle T\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
and the Wiener functional

\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}
with
\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon,i}\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty},\;1\leq i\leq N
,
and review an asymptotic expansion of the generalised expectation
\displaystyle{}_{-\infty}\langle T(F^{\varepsilon}),1\rangle_{\infty}=\mathbb{E}[\,T(F^{\varepsilon})\,]

using Malliavin-Watanabe calculus (
Watanabe 1987
). Now, suppose that
\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}
has the following asymptotic expansion. For any
\displaystyle J\in\mathbb{N}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}=\sum_{i=0}^{J}\varepsilon^{i}f_{i}+\widetilde{f}_{J+1,\varepsilon},

where
\displaystyle f_{i}\in\mathbb{D}^{\infty}
,
\displaystyle 0\leq i\leq J
and

\displaystyle\displaystyle\limsup_{\varepsilon\downarrow 0}\frac{\|\widetilde{f}_{J+1,\varepsilon}\|_{k,p}}{\varepsilon^{J+1}}<\infty,

for any
\displaystyle p\in(1,\infty)
and
\displaystyle k\in\mathbb{N}
.
Furthermore, we assume that
\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}
is uniformly non-degenerate with respect to
\displaystyle\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, i.e.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{\varepsilon\in(0,1)}\|(\det\sigma^{F^{\varepsilon}})^{-1}\|_{p}<\infty,

for all
\displaystyle p\in(1,\infty)
, where we recall that
\displaystyle\sigma^{F^{\varepsilon}}
is the Malliavin covariance of
\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}
. According to
Ikeda and Watanabe 2014
, we have an asymptotic expansion of
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\,T(F^{\varepsilon})\,]
as follows.
For any
\displaystyle J\in\mathbb{N}
, it holds

\displaystyle\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\,T(F^{\varepsilon})\,]=\sum_{i=0}^{J}\varepsilon^{i}\cdot\mathbb{E}[\Phi_{i}]+R_{J+1,\varepsilon},

(E.6)

where
\displaystyle\Phi_{i}\in\mathbb{D}^{-\infty},\;0\leq i\leq J
and

\displaystyle|R_{J+1,\varepsilon}|\leq C\varepsilon^{J+1}
for some positive constant
\displaystyle C>0
. In particular,
\displaystyle\Phi_{i}\in{\mathbb{D}}^{-\infty},\;0\leq i\leq J
are determined by the formal Taylor expansion, e.g.

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Phi_{0}=T(f_{0}),\qquad\Phi_{1}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}f_{1}^{i}(\partial_{i}T)(f_{0});

\displaystyle\displaystyle\Phi_{2}=\sum_{i=1}^{N}f_{2}^{i}(\partial_{i}T)(f_{0})+\frac{1}{2!}\sum_{i_{1},i_{2}=1}^{N}f_{1}^{i_{1}}f_{1}^{i_{2}}(\partial_{i_{1}}\partial_{i_{2}}T)(f_{0}).

We note that in the case of
\displaystyle T=\delta_{y}\in\mathscr{S}^{\prime}(\mathbb{R}^{N})
, i.e. the Dirac measure mass at
\displaystyle y\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
, (
E.6
) yields the asymptotic expansion for the Lebesgue density of the law of
\displaystyle F^{\varepsilon}
, i.e.
\displaystyle p^{F^{\varepsilon}}(y)=\mathbb{E}[\,\delta_{y}(F^{\varepsilon})\,]
. Via the Malliavin integration by parts, the remainder term
\displaystyle R_{J+1,\varepsilon}
is bounded by some constant
\displaystyle C>0
independent of
\displaystyle\delta_{y}
, typically of
\displaystyle y\in\mathbb{R}^{N}
.

E.2
Proof of Lemma
3.1

E.2.1
Step 1. Asymptotic Expansion

We first recall the definition of random variables
\displaystyle\bar{X}_{\Delta}^{\varepsilon,x}
in (
3.3
) in the main text. We introduce:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}=m_{x,\theta,\varepsilon}(\bar{X}^{\varepsilon,x}_{1})\equiv\widetilde{Y}+\sum_{l=1}^{3}\varepsilon^{l}\cdot\widetilde{Y}^{(l)},

(E.7)

where we have set for
\displaystyle y=[y_{R}^{\top},y_{S}^{\top}]^{\top}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,

\displaystyle\displaystyle m_{x,\theta,\varepsilon}(y):=\left[\begin{array}[]{c}\frac{{y_{R}-x_{R}-\varepsilon^{2}V_{R,0}(x,\beta)}}{\varepsilon}\\
\frac{y_{S}-x_{S}-\varepsilon^{2}V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)-\tfrac{\varepsilon^{4}}{2}\hat{V}_{0}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)}{\varepsilon^{3}}\end{array}\right],

and
\displaystyle\widetilde{Y},\,\widetilde{Y}^{(l)},\,1\leq l\leq 3
are explicitly given as:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{Y}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\widetilde{Y}_{R}\\[2.84544pt]
\widetilde{Y}_{S}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{k=1}^{d_{R}}V_{R,k}(x,\beta)B_{k,1}\\[2.84544pt]
\sum_{k=1}^{d_{R}}\hat{V}_{k}V_{S,0}(x,\gamma)\zeta_{k0,1}\end{bmatrix},\ \ \widetilde{Y}^{(1)}=\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=1}^{d_{R}}\hat{V}_{k_{1}}V_{R,k_{2}}(x,\theta)\zeta_{k_{1}k_{2},1}\\[2.84544pt]
\sum_{k_{1},k_{2}=1}^{d_{R}}\hat{V}_{k_{1}}\hat{V}_{k_{2}}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)\eta_{k_{1}k_{2},1}\end{bmatrix};

\displaystyle\displaystyle\widetilde{Y}^{(2)}

\displaystyle\displaystyle=\begin{bmatrix}\sum_{k=1}^{d_{R}}\bigl\{\hat{V}_{0}V_{R,k}(x,\theta)\zeta_{0k,1}+\hat{V}_{k}V_{R,0}(x,\theta)\zeta_{k0,1}\bigr\}\\[5.69046pt]
\sum_{k=1}^{d_{R}}\bigl\{\hat{V}_{0}\hat{V}_{k}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)\eta_{0k,1}+\hat{V}_{k}\hat{V}_{0}V_{S,0}(x,\theta)\eta_{k0,1}\bigr\}\end{bmatrix},\ \ \widetilde{Y}^{(3)}=\begin{bmatrix}\tfrac{1}{2}\hat{V}_{0}V_{R,0}(x,\theta)\\[2.84544pt]
\mathbf{0}_{d_{S}}\end{bmatrix}.

Transformation of random variables with
\displaystyle\varepsilon=\sqrt{\Delta}
yields:

\displaystyle\displaystyle p_{\Delta}^{\bar{X}}(x,y;\theta)=\mathbb{P}_{\theta}\big[\bar{X}_{1}^{\sqrt{\Delta},x}\in dy\big]/dy=\frac{1}{\sqrt{\Delta^{d_{R}+3d_{S}}}}\,p^{\widetilde{Y}^{{\sqrt{\Delta}}}}(\xi;\theta)|_{\xi=m_{x,\theta,{\sqrt{\Delta}}}(y)},

(E.8)

and thus we consider the asymptotic expansion of

\displaystyle\textstyle{p^{\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}}(\xi;\theta)=\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\delta_{\xi}(\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon})]}

with respect to
\displaystyle\varepsilon
.
The expansion is justified by the following result, with its proof contained in Section
E.3.1
.

Lemma E.1
.

Under conditions (
H1
)–(
H4
), the family of variables
\displaystyle\{\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}\}_{\varepsilon\in(0,1)}
is uniformly non-degenerate, i.e. for any
\displaystyle(x,\theta)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\Theta
, it holds:

\displaystyle\displaystyle\sup_{\varepsilon\in(0,1)}\|(\det\sigma^{\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}})^{-1}\|_{p}<\infty,\ \

for all
\displaystyle p\in(1,\infty)
, where
\displaystyle\sigma^{\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}}
is the Malliavin covariance of
\displaystyle\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}
.

Using Lemma
E.1
and (
E.6
), we get the following expansion of

\displaystyle p^{\widetilde{Y}^{\varepsilon}}(\xi;\theta)
: for
